//! Operator preflight runs the real activation on a private disk-backed snapshot.
// REL3 owns dormant-visibility counts here; SURF clause narration lives in release_narration.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#92-exact-preview-and-operator-vocabulary — no live pointer, generation or audit changes.
use super::*;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
struct Snapshot(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Snapshot {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

pub(super) fn preview(
    store_path: &Path,
    root: PathBuf,
    release: u64,
    options: &Options,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let bound = super::releases::bound_at(root.clone())?;
    let project = Project { root };
    let native = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project,&bound.schema)?;
    let id = snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default())
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let snapshot = Snapshot(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-release-preview-{id}")));
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&snapshot.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let path = snapshot.0.join("store.sqlite");
    // Probe the existing filesystem lock, independently of any HTTP address
    // hint. Keep a stopped store excluded through preview; never create a lock
    // sidecar or mutate its address record just to inspect ownership.
    let (live,_stopped_guard)=if store_path.exists() {
        let database=store_path.canonicalize().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        match snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(&database,&snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&database),false,0) {
            Ok(owner)=>match owner.try_lock() {
                Ok(())=>(false,Some(owner)),
                Err(std::fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock)=>(true,None),
                Err(std::fs::TryLockError::Error(e))=>return Err(format!("preview owner probe failed: {e}")),
            },
            Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>(false,None),
            Err(e)=>return Err(format!("preview owner probe failed: {e}")),
        }
    }else{(false,None)};
    // SQLite VACUUM INTO takes a coherent read snapshot even with a live WAL writer.
    // Busy/disk errors refuse this preview; never fall back to copying database bytes.
    if store_path.exists() {
    let source = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(store_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    source
        .conn()
        .execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()])
        .map_err(|e| {
            format!("snapshot preview failed (retry after store/disk becomes available): {e}")
        })?;
    drop(source);
    }
    let mut store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let held = store
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — preview uses the owning host's authoritative time, without minting or advancing anything.
    let activation_now=if live {
        let status=crate::release_control::request(store_path.parent().ok_or("preview store has no deployment directory")?,crate::release_control::Command::Status{after:0,items_after:None,event:None})?;
        if status["revision"]!=held["revision"] {return Err("preview raced release activation; retry against the current store".into());}
        status["clock"]["now"].as_i64().ok_or("serving owner does not expose its activation clock; restart it with this CLI before preview")?
    }else{snapback4_core::now_ms()};
    let mut activation =
        snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(release, held["release"].as_u64());
    activation.expected_revision = held["revision"].as_u64();
    activation.native_digest = native.installed.map(|n| n.digest);
    activation.operation = "preview".into();
    activation.restore_dormant = !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") || options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant");
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    let estimate = store
        .adoption_estimate(&bound.schema)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .map(|e| e.summary());
    let candidate_schema=bound.schema.clone();
    let candidate_programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|(p.program.name.clone(),p.program.clone())).collect();
    let mut hazards=Vec::new();
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    let prior:Option<(String,String)>=store.conn().query_row("SELECT schema,programs FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?))).ok();
    let prior_schema=prior.as_ref().map(|(schema,_)|serde_json::from_str::<snapback4_core::Schema>(schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).transpose()?;
    if let Some((schema,programs))=prior {
        let prior:snapback4_core::Schema=serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        steps=snapback4_core::successor::classify(&prior,&candidate_schema);
        let programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::Program>=serde_json::from_str(&programs).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = format!("release {release}");
        let compatibility = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &prior, &candidate_schema, held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0), &target_label, options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
        for line in compatibility {
            if let Some(warning) = line.strip_prefix("WARNING ") { hazards.push(warning.to_owned()); }
            else { notes.push(line.trim_start_matches("note: ").to_owned()); }
        }
        let rollback=!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction");
        let (behavior_warnings, behavior_notes) = super::release_narration::behavior(store_path, &store, &prior, &candidate_schema, &programs, &candidate_programs, &project, held["release"].as_u64(), &target_label, rollback)?;
        hazards.extend(behavior_warnings); notes.extend(behavior_notes);
        for name in prior.tables.keys().filter(|name|!candidate_schema.tables.contains_key(*name) && prior.maintain_for(name).is_none() && prior.tables[*name].ephemeral.is_none()) {
            let count:u64=store.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if count>0 {hazards.push(format!("table {name}: {count} rows become dormant; {target_label} lacks this table's behavior; preserve it in a bridge or accept its absence"));}
        }
    }
    super::release_safeguards::capture(&store)?;
    let mut engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared_at(
        store,
        bound.schema,
        bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
        &activation,
        options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
        activation_now,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut planned = engine
        .store_mut()
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    planned["items"] = serde_json::to_value(
        engine
            .store_mut()
            .dormant_inventory(None, usize::MAX)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let items: Vec<snapback4_core::store::DormantItem> = serde_json::from_value(planned["items"].clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut effects = super::release_safeguards::warnings(engine.store(),prior_schema.as_ref(),&candidate_schema,&candidate_programs,&items,release,!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
    effects.append(&mut hazards); hazards=effects;
    for fill in &engine.store().adoption_report.backfills { notes.push(format!("backfill: {fill}; confirm these historical values before selecting release {release}")); }
    steps.retain(|step|!step.message.contains("engine capture"));
    for step in &mut steps {
        if step.change==snapback4_core::successor::Change::Narrowing {
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
            step.message=format!("{}; active rows satisfy the candidate after dormant projection",step.message.split(';').next().unwrap_or(&step.message));
        }
        if let Some(position)=step.path.strip_prefix("maintains[").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix(']')).and_then(|s|s.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
            let label=candidate_schema.maintains[position].label();
            let visits=engine.store().adoption_report.views.iter().find(|view|view.name==label).map(|v|v.rows).unwrap_or(0);
            step.message=format!("maintain {label}: rebuilt from {visits} source rows");
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
        }
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path).replace(".columns.",".");
        let count=planned["items"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().find(|i|i["path"].as_str()==Some(path.as_str())).and_then(|i|i["rows"].as_u64()).unwrap_or(0);
        let unit = if step.path.contains(".columns.") { "cells" } else if step.path.contains(".indexes.") { "entries" } else { "rows" };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — per-path prose uses the same disjoint counts as the inventory totals.
        let retained = if count == 0 && unit == "cells" {
            format!("no standalone {unit} become dormant (cells of any dormant rows are counted with those rows)")
        } else if count == 0 {
            format!("no {unit} become dormant")
        } else {
            format!("its {unit} become dormant ({count} {unit}; restored on forward)")
        };
        step.message=step.message.replace("its rows are lost", &retained).replace("its values are lost", &retained);
    }
    let checklist = super::release_bridge::checklist(held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),release,&hazards);
    if let Some(path) = options.flags.get("bridge") {
        let current = held["release"].as_u64().ok_or("a bridge needs a current deployed release")?;
        super::release_bridge::write(Path::new(path),store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?,current,release,&checklist)?;
        eprintln!("bridge starter written to {path}; edit its current source and follow ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md before activation");
    }
    if options.json {
        println!("{}",serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({"preview":true,"checklist":checklist,"hazards":hazards,"notes":notes,"current":held,"plan":planned,"steps":steps,"estimate":estimate,"phases":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills})).unwrap());
    } else {
        println!("preview: release {release} would be current as generation {}; live release unchanged", engine.generation());
        super::release_summary::print(prior_schema.as_ref(), &candidate_schema, &candidate_programs, &hazards, &notes, &steps, &items);
        if !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") { println!("  checklist: prove the named refusal cases and affected lists/counts as real accounts before release {release}; `--bridge <new-dir>` saves current source plus this app's ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md"); }
        println!("  storage: {}",snapback4_core::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items));
        if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {
            for hazard in hazards { println!("  detail: WARNING {hazard}"); }
            for note in notes { println!("  detail: note: {note}"); }
            for step in steps {println!("  {}: {}",step.path,step.message);}
            for item in &items { println!("  {}: {} {} dormant (restored on compatible forward); sample ids [{}]", item.key, item.rows, item.unit(), item.sample_ids.join(", ")); }
            println!("{}", engine.store().adoption_report.summary());
            if let Some(estimate) = estimate {
                println!("{estimate}");
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

//! Owner-mediated release publication in the running HTTP process.
use super::*;
use crate::release_control::Command;
#[cfg(unix)]
use crate::release_control::Listener;
use snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation;

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(super) fn start(
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    dir: std::path::PathBuf,
) -> Result<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>, String> {
    let store = shared
        .owner
        .as_ref()
        .ok_or("release control requires a persisted owner")?
        .path()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let listener = Listener::bind(&dir, store)?;
    crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
    crate::lifecycle::background("release-control", move || {
        listener.run(|command| apply(&shared, &dir, command))
    })
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — only assignments and committed rebind follow the database commit.
pub(super) fn apply(
    shared: &Shared,
    dir: &std::path::Path,
    command: Command,
) -> Result<Json, String> {
    match command {
        Command::Status {
            after,
            items_after,
            event,
        } => {
            let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);
            let mut status=engine.store_mut().release_status(after,items_after.as_deref(),event).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            status["clock"]=json!({"now":shared.now(),"offsetMs":lock(&shared.clock).offset()});
            Ok(status)
        },
        Command::Prune {
            confirm,
            expected_revision,
        } => {
            let mut result = lock(&shared.engine)
                .store_mut()
                .prune_dormant(confirm, Some(expected_revision))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if let Some(items) = result["items"].as_array_mut() {
                items.truncate(16);
            }
            result["items_after"] = result["items"]
                .as_array()
                .and_then(|items| items.last())
                .map(|i| i["key"].clone())
                .unwrap_or(Json::Null);
            Ok(result)
        }
        Command::Activate {
            release,
            expected_release,
            expected_revision,
            digest,
            rollback,
            restore_dormant,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        } => {
            if release == 0 {
                return Err("release id must be positive".into());
            }
            let project = Project {
                root: dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string()),
            };
            if !std::fs::symlink_metadata(&project.root)
                .is_ok_and(|m| m.is_dir() && !m.file_type().is_symlink())
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} is not an immutable release directory"
                ));
            }
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("staged release content changed; redeploy its exact source".into());
            }
            if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release"
                ));
            }
            let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
                return Err(bound
                    .diagnostics
                    .iter()
                    .map(ToString::to_string)
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join("\n"));
            }
            let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
            crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("release artifacts changed during preparation; retry after inspecting immutable source".into());
            }
            crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
            let mut pointer =
                crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, expected_release, rollback)?;
            let options = ReleaseActivation {
                release,
                expected_previous: expected_release,
                expected_revision: Some(expected_revision),
                restore_dormant,
                native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
                operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
            };
            let _requests=shared.release_requests.write();
            let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
            lock(&shared.readers).begin_adoption(&bound);
            let generation = match engine.adopt_release_at(
                bound.schema.clone(),
                bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
                &options,
                allow_feed_adoption,
                shared.now(),
            ) {
                Ok(generation) => generation,
                Err(error) => {
                    lock(&shared.readers).finish_adoption();
                    return Err(error.to_string());
                }
            };
            #[cfg(test)]
            test_work(&shared.release_committed);
            engine.set_prepared_native(prepared.module);
            engine.set_presence(Some(shared.presence.clone()));
            shared.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, shared.now());
            lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            {
                let mut active = lock(&shared.bound);
                let mut advertised = lock(&shared.generation);
                let mut native = lock(&shared.native);
                shared.latency.adopt(&bound);
                *active = bound;
                *advertised = generation;
                *native = prepared.installed;
                let mut root = lock(&shared.release_root);
                *root = Some(project.root);
                shared.worker_revision.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Release);
            }
            crate::lifecycle::generation(generation);
            shared.publish_engine(&engine);
            // Completion I/O can fail, but never abandons the new runtime.
            pointer.finish(generation)?;
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 serve: release {release} activated without restart as generation {generation}"
            );
            Ok(
                json!({"release":release,"generation":generation,"revision":expected_revision+1,"hot":true,"adoption":engine.store().adoption_report.compact(),"details":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills}),
            )
        }
    }
}
//! Server activation builder; all fallible work precedes commit.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    pub(super) fn activate(
        store: &mut Store,
        schema: &Schema,
        programs: &BTreeMap<String, Program>,
        release: Option<&release::ReleaseActivation>,
        now: i64,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_backend: Option<&crate::store::opening::Backend>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, crate::store::opening::Metadata), RuntimeError> {
        schema.validate_column_owners()?;
        for program in programs.values() {
            crate::schema::ephemeral::admit_program(schema, program)?;
            admission::check_state_predicates(schema, program)?;
        }
        for program in programs.values() {
            for table in crate::ir::read_sites(program)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|s| s.table)
                .chain(crate::ir::write_tables(program).into_iter().map(|(t, _)| t))
            {
                schema.refuse_private(&table)?;
            }
        }
        for schedule in &schema.schedules {
            if !schedule.valid()
            {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} has an invalid period or offset",
                    schedule.name
                )));
            }
            let Some(program) = programs
                .get(&schedule.target)
                .filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
            else {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore or remove the schedule",
                    schedule.name, schedule.target
                )));
            };
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let seat = schedule
                    .service
                    .as_deref()
                    .filter(|seat| crate::identity::is_service(seat))
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        RuntimeError::Schema(
                            "a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                actions::authorize(program, seat)?;
            } else if schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "a scheduled job cannot name a service seat".into(),
                ));
            }
            if program.args.iter().any(|(name, kind)| {
                !matches!(name.as_str(), "periodStart" | "periodEnd")
                    || *kind != crate::ir::ArgType::Time
            }) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "scheduled work takes periodStart: time and periodEnd: time, or nothing".into(),
                ));
            }
        }
        let prior_items = std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new());
        let population=std::cell::Cell::new(None);
        let outcome=store.activate_at_with_launch(schema, Some(programs), false, now, allow_feed_adoption, |conn| {
            Store::check_selected_release(conn, expected_release)?;
            if let Some(backend) = expected_backend { backend.check(conn)?; }
            if let Some(options)=release {
                options.check(conn)?;
                *prior_items.borrow_mut()=crate::store::release_history::inventory(conn,None,usize::MAX)?;
                crate::store::dormant::enable(conn)?;
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('release_native_digest',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[options.native_digest.as_deref().unwrap_or("")])?;
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('activation_restore',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[if options.restore_dormant {"true"}else{"false"}])?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx, changed, report| {
            let now=now.max(tx.time_audience_clock()?);
            if let Some(options)=release {tx.set_meta("release",&options.release.to_string())?;}
            use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;
            let repair_live_sums = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'live_sum_candidates'", [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.as_deref() != Some("1");
            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?;
            let needs_maintained_id_marker = (schema.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())
                || previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())))
                && tx.meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META)?.as_deref() != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
            if !changed && !repair_live_sums && !needs_maintained_id_marker { return Ok(()); }
            let repair_maintained_ids = needs_maintained_id_marker
                && previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none()));
            if changed {
                let epoch = tx.next_seq()?;
                for table in partition::changed_group_audiences(previous.as_ref(), schema) {
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_key(&table), &epoch.to_string())?;
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_time_key(&table), &now.to_string())?;
                }
            }
            let population_changed=tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true");
            let rebuild: Vec<_> = schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| {
                (changed && (population_changed || previous.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| crate::successor::maintain_rebuild_needed(old, schema, m))))
                    || (repair_maintained_ids && m.row_key.is_none())
                    || (repair_live_sums && matches!(m.kind, crate::schema::MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && schema.table(&m.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring()))
            }).collect();
            tx.set_meta("live_sum_candidates", "1")?;
            if needs_maintained_id_marker {
                // This write is inside the activation transaction: a failed
                // rebuild leaves both old rows and the old/missing marker. A
                // first aggregate has no legacy target identity to rewrite,
                // but records the current address function for later opens.
                tx.set_meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META, crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION)?;
            }
            let attribution = recorded::paths_changed(schema, previous.as_ref());
            let columns = column_activation::changed_parents(schema, if population_changed {None} else {previous.as_ref()});
            if rebuild.is_empty() && !attribution && columns.is_empty() && !repair_maintained_ids { return Ok(()); }
            let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
            let phase = std::time::Instant::now();
            if attribution {
                // This runner owns only activation rewrites; ordinary rebuilds
                // below retain their commit and partition accounting.
                let mut capture = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
                capture.capture_activation(previous.as_ref(), Some(report))?;
                report.detail("capture recorded paths", phase);
                // A newly introduced aggregate has no legacy identity map to
                // preserve. Keep its event-free recorded-path rebuild out of
                // commit accounting, as activation did before the address
                // migration existed.
                if !repair_maintained_ids {
                    capture.rebuild_recorded_activation(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild.iter().copied().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), report)?;
                }
            }
            let mut runner = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
            if repair_maintained_ids {
                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
                let rewritten = runner.rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(&rebuild.iter().copied().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>(), previous.as_ref(), Some(tx))?;
                if rewritten != 0 { report.changes.push(format!("rewrote {rewritten} legacy aggregate references")); }
                report.detail("rewrite legacy aggregate references", started);
            }
            // Legacy maps must be captured before any activation rebuild can
            // remove their target rows. Recorded values themselves are
            // stamped first so their historical paths may still traverse the
            // predecessor addresses.
            if attribution && repair_maintained_ids {
                runner.rebuild_recorded_activation(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild.iter().copied().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), report)?;
            }
            runner.activate_columns(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild, &columns, Some(report))?;
            for maintain in rebuild.into_iter().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()) {
                if !recorded::table_paths_changed(schema.table(&maintain.source), previous.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.table(&maintain.source))) {
                    runner.rebuild_maintain_reported(maintain, report)?;
                }
            }
            let events = runner.events.take();
            let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
            let written = runner.rows_written;
            drop(runner);
            if written > 0 || !columns.is_empty() {
                tx.record_commit(seq, now, "rebuild", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "rebuild", &tables, written)?;
                partition::append(tx, seq, &events)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx| {
            if let Some(options)=release {
                crate::store::release_history::finalize_restorations(tx)?;
                let mut items=crate::store::release_history::inventory(tx.conn(),None,usize::MAX)?;
                let dormant_items=items.len();
                let mut restored_items=0usize;
                for item in &mut items {
                    let before=prior_items.borrow().iter().find(|i|i.key==item.key).cloned();
                    let count=before.as_ref().map_or(0,|i|i.rows);
                    item.before_rows=Some(count);item.after_rows=Some(item.rows);
                    item.before_sample_ids=Some(before.map_or_else(Vec::new,|i|i.sample_ids));
                    let restored=if item.kind=="rows" {
                        tx.meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else if item.kind=="column" {tx.meta(&format!("activation_column_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)} else {0};
                    item.restored_rows=Some(restored);
                    if restored>0 {item.action="partially restored".into();restored_items+=1;}
                }
                let remaining:std::collections::BTreeSet<_>=items.iter().map(|i|i.key.clone()).collect();
                for mut item in prior_items.borrow().iter().filter(|i|!remaining.contains(&i.key)).cloned() {
                    let restored=if matches!(item.kind.as_str(),"rows"|"column") {
                        let prefix=if item.kind=="rows" {"activation_narrowed_restored"} else {"activation_column_restored"};
                        tx.meta(&format!("{prefix}:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else if item.kind=="table" {
                        tx.meta(&format!("activation_table_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else {item.rows};
                    item.action=if restored>0 || item.definition_only {"restored"} else {"removed without restoration"}.into();
                    item.before_rows=Some(item.rows);item.after_rows=Some(0);item.restored_rows=Some(restored);item.before_sample_ids=Some(item.sample_ids.clone());
                    if restored>0 || item.definition_only {restored_items+=1;}
                    items.push(item);
                }
                population.set(Some((tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0))));
                crate::store::release_history::event(tx,&options.operation,Some(options.release),options.expected_previous,serde_json::json!({"dormant_items":dormant_items,"restored_items":restored_items,"native_digest":options.native_digest,"narrowed_retained":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"narrowed_restored":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)}),&items)?;
                tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key IN ('activation_restore','activation_population','activation_narrowed_retained','activation_narrowed_restored') OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%'",[])?;
            }
            crate::store::opening::Metadata::read(tx)
        });
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Some((retained,restored))=population.get().filter(|(a,b)|*a>0 || *b>0) {
                store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0,format!("{retained} newly dormant {}; {restored} narrowed {} restored", if retained == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }, if restored == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }));
            }
        }
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Ok(items)=store.dormant_inventory(None,usize::MAX) {
                if release.is_some() { store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0, crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items)); }
            }
        }
        outcome
    }
}
//! Human preview groups facts about one affected table/operation once.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#15-wave-15-preview-and-rollback-day5 — full evidence remains available in JSON and verbose output.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use snapback4_core::{Schema, ir::Program, store::DormantItem, successor::Step};
struct Group { warnings: Vec<String>, notes: Vec<String>, priority: u8 }
impl Default for Group { fn default() -> Self {Self {warnings:Vec::new(),notes:Vec::new(),priority:2}} }
fn table_prefix(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
    tables.iter().filter(|name|text.strip_prefix(name.as_str()).is_some_and(|tail|tail.is_empty() || tail.starts_with(['.',':',' ']))).max_by_key(|name|name.len()).cloned()
}
fn subject(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>) -> String {
    // Read only generated subject positions, never arbitrary words inside an
    // authored predicate/refusal/value. Unknown formats remain release facts.
    if let Some(table)=table_prefix(text,tables) {return table;}
    for marker in ["table ","dependent ","backfill: "] {
        if let Some(tail)=text.strip_prefix(marker) && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    let prefix=text.split(';').next().unwrap_or(text);
    if text.starts_with("queries/counts ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" read ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("release ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" safety dependencies on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("rollback users get release ") {
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" quotas on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once("'s ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    if text.starts_with("release ") {
        if let Some(token)=prefix.split_whitespace().last() && let Some((owner,effect))=token.rsplit_once('.') {
            if effect=="require" {return format!("operation {owner}");}
            if tables.contains(owner) && matches!(effect,"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete") {return owner.into();}
        }
        for name in programs.keys() {
            if prefix.ends_with(&format!(" operation {name}")) {return format!("operation {name}");}
        }
    }
    "release behavior".into()
}
fn brief(text: &str) -> String {
    // Semicolons also separate authored guards and multiple cap declarations;
    // retaining the full fact avoids dropping their contracts or caveats.
    text.to_owned()
}
pub(super) fn print(current: Option<&Schema>, target: &Schema, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>, warnings: &[String], notes: &[String], steps: &[Step], items: &[DormantItem]) {
    let tables: BTreeSet<_>=target.tables.keys().chain(current.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.tables.keys())).cloned().collect();
    let mut groups=BTreeMap::<String,Group>::new();
    for (warning, lines) in [(true,warnings),(false,notes)] {
        for line in lines {
            let group=groups.entry(subject(line,&tables,programs)).or_default();
            let priority=if line.contains("safety guard") || line.contains(".require;") {0} else if line.contains("confidentiality") || line.contains("safety rule") {1} else {2};
            group.priority=group.priority.min(priority);
            let text=if priority==0 {line.clone()} else {brief(line)};
            let dest=if warning {&mut group.warnings} else {&mut group.notes};
            if !dest.contains(&text) {dest.push(text);}
        }
    }
    for step in steps {
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path);
        let key=table_prefix(path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||path.to_owned());
        if path.strip_prefix(&key).is_some_and(|tail|tail.starts_with(".indexes.")) {continue;}
        let group=groups.entry(key).or_default();
        // Keep each user-visible source change even when another fact already
        // names this table. Only index plumbing is verbose-only.
        let detail=brief(&format!("{}: {}",path.replace(".columns.","."),step.message));
        if !group.notes.contains(&detail) {group.notes.push(detail);}
    }
    let mut storage=BTreeMap::<String,[u64;3]>::new();
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.rows>0 && item.kind!="backfill") {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        let slot=match item.kind.as_str() {"column"=>1,"index"=>2,_=>0};
        storage.entry(name).or_default()[slot]+=item.rows;
    }
    for (name,[rows,cells,indexes]) in storage {
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {rows} dormant rows, {cells} cells, {indexes} index entries"));
    }
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.kind=="backfill" && item.rows>0) {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {} private backfill preimages ({} bytes)",item.rows,item.bytes));
    }
    let mut groups=groups.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    groups.sort_by_key(|(name,g)|(g.priority,name.clone()));
    for (name,mut group) in groups {
        let warning=!group.warnings.is_empty();
        group.warnings.append(&mut group.notes);
        println!("  {} {name}: {}{}",if warning {"WARNING"} else {"note:"},group.warnings.join("; "),if warning {"; verify these cases or preserve the current behavior in a bridge release"} else {""});
    }
}

#[test]
fn authored_values_cannot_assign_a_guard_to_an_unrelated_table() {
    let tables=BTreeSet::from(["unrelated".to_owned(),"workspace.plans".to_owned()]);
    let line="release 1 loosens book.require; old: require title != 'unrelated' else STOP; new: allow; rollback users lose a current safety guard";
    assert_eq!(subject(line,&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"operation book");
    assert_eq!(subject("workspace.plans.state: 2 rows become dormant",&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"workspace.plans");
}

//! Business-state regressions for declarative day-two adoption.
use super::*;
use crate::{Context,Engine,WriteState,wire};

use serde_json::{json,Value as Json};
fn compile(source:&str)->(Schema,Vec<crate::ir::Program>){
    let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
    (serde_json::from_str(&serde_json::to_string(&b.schema).unwrap()).unwrap(),b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|serde_json::from_str(&serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap()).unwrap()).collect())
}
fn engine(source:&str)->Engine{let(s,p)=compile(source);Engine::new_release(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,1,None).unwrap()}
fn adopt(e:&mut Engine,source:&str,n:u64)->Result<u64,RuntimeError>{
    let(s,p)=compile(source);let previous=e.store().meta("release").unwrap().map(|s|s.parse().unwrap());
    e.adopt_release(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(n,previous),false)
}

fn field(e:&mut Engine,table:&str,id:&str,c:&str)->Value{row(e,table,id).field(c).unwrap().clone()}

fn all_fifty_wave17_change_shapes_run_without_migration_programs(){
 let cases:Vec<Json>=serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../tests/backfills/wave17.json")).unwrap();assert_eq!(cases.len(),50);
 for case in cases {
  let lane=case["lane"].as_u64().unwrap();let inputs=case["inputs"].as_object().unwrap();let outputs=case["outputs"].as_object().unwrap();
  let columns=|fields:&serde_json::Map<String,Json>|fields.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("  {c}: {}\n",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<String>();
  let old=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n",columns(inputs));
  let args=inputs.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("{c}: {}",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
  let next=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill rows change{lane}({args}):\n  {}\n",columns(outputs),case["expression"].as_str().unwrap());
  let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),next.clone())]);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"lane {lane} {}: {:?}\n{next}",case["slug"],bound.diagnostics);
  let mut e=engine(&old);
  for (i,r) in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){let mut input=r["input"].as_object().unwrap().clone();input.insert("id".into(),json!(format!("r{i}")));seed(&mut e,"rows",Json::Object(input));}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane}: {error}\n{next}"));
  assert!(e.schema().public_projection().backfills.is_empty(),"historical transforms never enter device input");
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){
   let got=row(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"));
   for(c,wanted)in r["expect"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(got.field(c).unwrap()),*wanted,"lane {lane} row {i} column {c}");}
  }
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} rollback: {error}"));
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){for(c,wanted)in r["input"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(&field(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"),c)),*wanted,"lane {lane} restored {c}");}}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} forward: {error}"));
 }
}
#[test]
fn unique_lookup_uses_historical_rows_and_rechecks_changed_lookup_values(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  unique principal\n  read <- deny\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill bookings plot(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"A17"}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("A17".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 // Trusted seed replaces the source row as an operator update for this test.
 seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"B20"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("B20".into()));
}
#[test]
fn cross_reference_existence_is_evaluated_before_any_table_output_changes(){
 let old="table sightings:\n  tower: text <=20\n  reportedAt: time\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  tower:","  confidence: enum('confirmed','possible')\n  tower:")+"backfill sightings confidence(tower: text <=20, reportedAt: time):\n  {confidence:'confirmed' if exists sightings where .tower != tower and .reportedAt >= reportedAt - 15m and .reportedAt <= reportedAt + 15m else 'possible'}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"a","tower":"north","reportedAt":1000000}));seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"b","tower":"south","reportedAt":1100000}));seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"c","tower":"west","reportedAt":9000000}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","a","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","b","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","c","confidence"),Value::String("possible".into()));
}
#[test]
fn retained_table_reentry_never_feeds_a_transform_its_own_output(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for direct_old in [false,true] {
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();
  if direct_old {adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(3));}
  else {adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(3));}
 }
}
#[test]
fn previously_narrowed_rows_join_the_backfill_source_snapshot(){
 let old="table facts:\n  principal: principal\n  n: int 0..20\n  unique principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("int 0..20","int 0..5");
 let next=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int").replace("  owner: principal","  score: int\n  owner: principal")+"backfill facts twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\nbackfill bookings scores(owner: principal):\n  {score:(facts[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"facts",json!({"id":"a","principal":"user:a","n":9}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"b","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("facts").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"facts","a","doubled"),Value::Int(18));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","b","score"),Value::Int(9));
}
#[test]
fn historical_lookup_duplicates_refuse_before_another_patch_can_resolve_them(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  replacement: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  principal: principal","  unique principal\n  principal: principal").replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill members uniquePrincipals(replacement: principal):\n  {principal:replacement}\nbackfill bookings plots(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);for(id,replacement,plot)in[("a","user:a","A"),("b","user:b","B")]{seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":id,"principal":"user:x","replacement":replacement,"plot":plot}));}
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"c","owner":"user:x"}));let g=e.generation();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical lookup")&&error.contains("not unique")&&error.contains("2 affected rows"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn pruning_preimages_is_visible_and_exact_reversal_then_refuses(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 let preview=e.store_mut().prune_dormant(false,None).unwrap();assert!(preview["items"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|i|i["kind"]=="backfill"&&i["rows"]==1));
 e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();let error=adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("explicitly pruned"),"{error}");assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));
}
#[test]
fn carried_backfills_apply_to_rows_restored_for_the_first_time(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  label: enum('a','b')\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("enum('a','b')","enum('a')");
 let with_fill=narrow.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let restored=with_fill.replace("enum('a')","enum('a','b')");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":9,"label":"b"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&with_fill,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&restored,4).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(18));
}

fn identity_cannot_be_a_backfill_output() {

 assert!(!b.diagnostics.is_empty());
}
#[test]
fn pruned_active_membership_refuses_reentry_without_double_applying() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();
 let g=e.generation();let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("explicitly pruned"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn prune_drops_deleted_lifetime_receipts_before_id_reuse() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":10}));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(10));
}
#[test]
fn dropped_historical_reference_cannot_recapture_a_reused_parent() {
 let wide="table parents:\n  public 'fixture'\ntable children:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  status: enum('live','new')\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let mut e=engine(wide);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p"}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p","status":"new"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&wide.replace("enum('live','new')","enum('live')"),2).unwrap();
 let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}
 seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p"}));
 let next=wide.replace("  parent: parents on delete keep","  replacement: parents on delete keep")+"backfill children copy(parent: parents):\n  {replacement:parent}\n";
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}
#[test]
fn expired_unswept_source_cannot_extend_its_dropped_expiry() {
 let old="table items:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 for dormant in [false,true] {
  let mut e=engine(old);let r=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","until":50})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),r)],0).unwrap();
  if dormant {let(s,p)=compile("query empty():\n  return true\n");e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,1).unwrap();}
  let next=old.replace("  expire at .until\n","")+"backfill items prolong(until: time):\n  {until:until + 1d}\n";
  adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 }
}

#[test]
fn absent_optional_output_is_not_a_native_image_with_missing_historical_inputs() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  n: int","  derived: int ?\n  n: int")+"backfill items absent(missing: int):\n  {derived:missing}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));let g=e.generation();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical input items.missing"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn removal_of_last_opening_boundary_keeps_the_durable_retirement_floor() {
 let old="table items:\n  opens: time\n  until: time\n  n: int\n  not before .opens\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  not before .opens\n","").replace("  expire at .until\n","")+"backfill items prolong(until: time):\n  {until:until + 1d}\n";
 for requested in [1,100] {
  let(s,p)=compile(old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let r=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","opens":0,"until":50,"n":3})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),r)],0).unwrap();
  {let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();tx.pin_time_audience_clock(100).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,requested).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0,"source deadline before persisted floor, requested={requested}");
 }
}

#[test]
fn a_new_named_declaration_transforms_existing_historical_inputs() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let triple=twice.replace("twice(n:","triple(n:").replace("n * 2","n * 3");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&triple,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(9));
}
#[test]
fn dropped_required_and_optional_inputs_survive_active_table_reentry() {
 for kind in ["int","int ?"] {
  let old=format!("table items:\n  n: {kind}\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let next=old.replace(&format!("  n: {kind}"),"  doubled: int")+&format!("backfill items twice(n: {kind}):\n  {{doubled:(n ?? 0) * 2}}\n");
  let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));
 }
}
#[test]
fn reentry_restores_shared_snapshots_for_arguments_and_point_reads() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for later in [false,true] {
  let copied=twice.replace("  n: int","  copied: int\n  lookedUp: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items copy(n: int, id: items):\n  {copied:n, lookedUp:(items[id] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));
  if later {adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();}
  adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();let expected=if later {6}else{3};
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","lookedUp"),Value::Int(expected));
 }
}

#[test]
fn a_retained_transform_output_cannot_prove_native_origin() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let broken=twice.replace("twice(n: int)","other(missing: int)").replace("doubled:n * 2","doubled:missing");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&broken,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical input items.missing"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn partial_table_reentry_restores_carried_declarations_in_the_same_cohort() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let parent_fill="backfill parents twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  parent: parents","  copied: int\n  parent: parents")+parent_fill+"backfill children copy(parent: parents):\n  {copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":3}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,&(parents.to_owned()+parent_fill),3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"parents","p","n"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","copied"),Value::Int(3));
}

#[test]
fn leaving_the_last_backfill_cannot_remove_an_ended_source_lifetime() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  until: time\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  until: time","  until: time\n  expire at .until")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for absent in [false,true] {
  let(s,p)=compile(old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let r=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","n":3,"until":50})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),r)],0).unwrap();
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,1).unwrap();
  if absent {let(s,p)=compile("query empty():\n  return true\n");e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(3,Some(2)),false,2).unwrap();}
  let previous=if absent {3}else{2};let(s,p)=compile(old);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,Some(previous)),false,100).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 }
}
#[test]
fn reentry_keeps_absence_distinct_from_a_later_default() {
 let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  public","  n: int default 100\n  public")+"backfill items one(n: int ?):\n  {n:(n ?? 0) + 1}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(1));
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(1));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(1));
}
#[test]
fn saved_reference_inputs_keep_their_own_lifetime_after_live_retargeting() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let mutation="mutation retarget():\n  update children['c'] {parent:'q'}\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n"+mutation;
 let fill="backfill children copy(parent: parents):\n  {copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  parent: parents","  copied: int\n  parent: parents")+fill;
 let dropped=next.replace("  parent: parents on delete keep\n","").replace(mutation,"");
 let mut e=engine(&old);for(id,n)in[("p",3),("q",9)]{seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":id,"n":n}));}seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();write(&mut e,"retarget");adopt(&mut e,&dropped,3).unwrap();
 let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":77}));
 adopt(&mut e,parents,4).unwrap();let error=adopt(&mut e,&dropped,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn a_new_reference_output_keeps_its_parent_lifetime_when_the_row_narrows() {
 let old="table parents:\n  name: text <=10\n  unique name\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable children:\n  n: int 0..10\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  n: int 0..10","  link: parents\n  n: int 0..5")+"backfill children link():\n  {link:(parents[name = 'p'] ! else NOT_FOUND).id}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","name":"p"}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","n":9}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);
 let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","name":"p"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10"),3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0,"a retained output reference cannot recapture a reused parent");
}
#[test]
fn a_transformed_restored_row_keeps_its_new_cells_when_it_becomes_dormant_again() {
 let old="table parents:\n  n: int 0..10\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable children:\n  label: enum('a','b')\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("enum('a','b')","enum('a')");
 let next=old.replace("  n: int 0..10","  n: int 0..5").replace("  label:","  link: parents\n  label:")+"backfill children migration(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2, link:(parents['p'] ! else NOT_FOUND).id}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":9}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","label":"b","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&next.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10"),4).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","n"),Value::Int(6));
}

#[test]
fn native_images_survive_optional_missing_inputs_and_constant_backfills() {
 for optional in [false,true] {
  let old="table items:\n  n: int ?\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let next=old.replace("  n: int ?","  derived: int")+if optional {"backfill items derive(n: int ?):\n  {derived:(n ?? 0) * 2}\n"}else{"backfill items derive():\n  {derived:0}\n"};
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","derived":9}));
  adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
 }
}
#[test]
fn invalid_present_inputs_never_qualify_as_native_missing_inputs() {
 let v2="table items:\n  n: bool\n  derived: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=v2.replace("  derived: int\n","");
 let next=v2.to_owned()+"backfill items derive(n: int, missing: int ?):\n  {derived:n + (missing ?? 0)}\n";
 let mut e=engine(v2);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":true,"derived":9}));adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical input items.n"),"{error}");
}
#[test]
fn original_absence_survives_later_defaults_until_a_meaningful_input_edit() {
 let old="table items:\n  label: text <=10\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n";
 let fill="backfill items derive(n: int ?):\n  {derived:n ?? 0}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  label:","  derived: int\n  label:")+fill;
 let later=next.replace("  label:","  n: int default 100\n  label:")+"mutation edit(n: int):\n  update items['a'] {n}\nmutation label():\n  update items['a'] {label:'changed'}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","label":"old"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();write(&mut e,"label");
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(0));
 for n in [200,100] {let r=e.mutate("edit",None,Context::new("alice",std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("n".into(),Value::Int(n))]))).unwrap();assert!(matches!(r.state,WriteState::Sent{..}),"{r:?}");}
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(100));
}

#[test]
fn a_new_declaration_name_does_not_inherit_another_native_definition() {
 let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  public","  derived: int\n  public")+"backfill items zero():\n  {derived:0}\n";
 let newer=next.replace("items zero()","items one()").replace("derived:0","derived:1");
 let mut e=engine(old);adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","derived":9}));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&newer,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(1));
}

#[test]
fn native_authored_outputs_survive_matching_inputs_and_refuse_changed_inputs() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation edit():\n  update items['native'] {n:4}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  n: int","  derived: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {derived:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","n":3,"derived":9}));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();write(&mut e,"edit");let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("native backfill inputs changed"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn backfill_source_expiry_applies_cascade_set_null_and_restrict_atomically() {
 for action in ["cascade","set null","restrict"] {for restored in [false,true] {
  let optional=if action=="set null" {" ?"}else{""};
  let old=format!("table parents:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable children:\n  parent: parents{optional} on delete {action}\n  cap 4 by parent\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let next=old.replace("table items:\n  n:","table items:\n  doubled: int\n  n:")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
  let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let rows=[("parents",json!({"id":"p","until":50})),("children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"})),("items",json!({"id":"i","n":3}))];
  let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|(t,r)|(t.to_owned(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[t],&r).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>();e.seed_at(rows,0).unwrap();
  if restored {let(s,p)=compile("table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,1).unwrap();}
  let g=e.generation();let(s,p)=compile(&next);let result=e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(3,Some(if restored {2}else{1})),false,100);
  if action=="restrict" {let error=result.unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("source expiry")&&error.contains("restrict"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());assert!(row(&mut e,"items","i").field("doubled").is_err());}
  else {result.unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),0);if action=="cascade" {assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);}else{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","parent"),Value::Null);}assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","i","doubled"),Value::Int(6));}
 }}
}
#[test]
fn reset_rebuilds_retained_schema_metadata_before_native_inserts_or_reseeding() {
 for reseed in [false,true] {
  let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let next=old.replace("  n: int","  derived: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {derived:n * 2}\n";
  let mut e=engine(old);adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
  let native=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"native","n":3,"derived":9})).unwrap();
  e.dev_reset_at(if reseed {vec![("items".into(),native)]}else{vec![]},crate::now_ms()).unwrap();
  if !reseed {seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","n":3,"derived":9}));}
  adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
 }
}

#[test]
fn enum_replacement_accepts_original_preimages_and_preserves_native_outputs() {
 let old="table cues:\n  status: enum('standby','done')\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let second=old.replace("enum('standby','done')","enum('standby','complete','skipped')")+"backfill cues complete(status: enum('standby','done')):\n  {status:'complete' if status = 'done' else status}\n";
 let third=old.replace("enum('standby','done')","enum('standby','finished','skipped')")+"backfill cues finished(status: enum('standby','done','complete','skipped')):\n  {status:'finished' if status = 'done' or status = 'complete' else status}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"cues",json!({"id":"a","status":"done"}));adopt(&mut e,&second,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"cues",json!({"id":"native","status":"complete"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&third,3).unwrap();for id in ["a","native"]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues",id,"status"),Value::String("finished".into()));}
 adopt(&mut e,&second,2).unwrap();for id in ["a","native"]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues",id,"status"),Value::String("complete".into()));}
}

#[test]
fn retained_hidden_optional_inputs_are_used_when_reentering_a_backfill() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int ?\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next="table items:\n  derived: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill items twice(n: int ?):\n  {derived:(n ?? 0) * 2}\n";
 let bridge="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(6));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,bridge,3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(6));
}
#[test]
fn source_expiry_observes_children_removed_in_the_same_release() {
 for action in ["cascade","set null","restrict"] {
  let optional=if action=="set null" {" ?"}else{""};
  let parent="table parents:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let child=format!("table children:\n  parent: parents{optional} on delete {action}\n  cap 4 by parent\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let item="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let old=format!("{parent}{child}{item}");let next=format!("{parent}{}backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {{doubled:n * 2}}\n",item.replace("  n:","  doubled: int\n  n:"));
  let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let rows=[("parents",json!({"id":"p","until":50})),("children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"})),("items",json!({"id":"i","n":3}))];
  let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|(t,r)|(t.to_owned(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[t],&r).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>();e.seed_at(rows,0).unwrap();
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);let result=e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,100);
  if action=="restrict" {let error=result.unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("restrict"),"{error}");assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),1);}else{
   result.unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),0);
   let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let t=tx.num("children").unwrap();
   let saved:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id='c'",[t],|r|r.get(0)).optional().unwrap();
   if action=="cascade" {assert!(saved.is_none());}else{let saved:Value=serde_json::from_str(&saved.unwrap()).unwrap();assert_eq!(*saved.field("parent").unwrap(),Value::Null);}
  }
 }
}
